| 0.211.0 | 2026-09-10 | A row names the build, upstream and routing it ran on, and a trial its own budget (D174, amends D159; Refs #100). `yi --version` printed the workspace's `0.2.0` on every build, so rows 0023, 0025 and 0026 hashed one `yiVersion`; `crates/cli/build.rs` now stamps the `version:` line of docs/ARCHITECTURE.md, and a tree without that line does not build. Every OpenRouter chunk names the upstream that served it, and row 0028 paid exactly twice the catalog on an upstream nothing kept: the completions mapper keeps the name, or a settled turn's `provider_name`, as one `upstream` diagnostic per message, `yi_usage.upstreams` tallies `byUpstream` into the harbor metadata and run.py's `row.json`, and `evals/axes.py` prints a new `upstreams` cell (`Z.AI=300, DeepInfra=21`; `docs/eval-ledger.md` gains the column). `EVAL_ROUTING`, a JSON object, goes verbatim into the trial config's `routing` key through `yi_usage.eval_config`, the one writer for `evals/run.py` and the harbor adapter, and rides the fingerprint's mode as `+routing{…}`; anything else is refused, as is `--home` on the fixtures lane, whose config stays the caller's. The harbor adapter rewrites the task's `You have 28800 seconds` to the multiplier's share, the number `--deadline` already carried, and the event `tee` appends, so a resumed trial keeps its first segment. The D159 row and ADR name 0.190.0's deprioritisers where they still said `sort: throughput`. Tests: `cli_surfaces::version_prints_the_architecture_version`, `openrouter::a_chunk_that_names_its_upstream_leaves_one_upstream_diagnostic`, `settle::tests::a_generation_record_becomes_a_measured_usage`; `evals/selftest.py` `check_command`, `check_usage`, `check_budget_sentence`, `check_eval_config`, `check_axes`. growth +14545: measured at the push under D174; this version adds about 30 src lines. |
| 0.210.0 | 2026-09-10 | The ledger row says what it could not measure (D173, amends D140; Refs #100). `evals/axes.py` dropped a pass@k CI of exactly 0.0 as falsy, so rows 0023 and 0025 printed no width; the row now prints `±0.0`. A harbor trial whose verifier started and returned no result under a timeout is `verifierUnmeasured`: photonic's verifier overran its 300 s ceiling after the agent timed out, harbor kept the agent timeout, and the trial scored 0.0 like a verified failure. `timedOut` is the agent's timeout alone; any other harbor exception (a missing reward file, a setup timeout) and a run.py row with a non-zero exit are `errored`. Two cells join the row on the right, `timeouts` (`agent/verifier/error`) and `partials` (`Σpassed/Σtests` over the verifiers' `ctrf.json`, then the mean `partial_score` of any `trace_results.json`); on the 2026-09-09 slice archive they read `4/2/0` and `222/276 0.53`, figures the ledger had transcribed by hand. `docs/eval-ledger.md` gains both columns and says `tokens` prints uncached input. Tests: `evals/selftest.py check_axes` (a timed-out harbor fixture trial with a masked verifier, `ctrf.json` and `trace_results.json`; a both-pass k=2 row; a run that exited 1; a verifier timeout, a missing reward file and a setup timeout, each counted in one cell). growth +14513: measured at the push under D173; this version adds no src lines. |
| 0.207.0 | 2026-09-10 | `read` converts office documents and PDFs through the kernel venv (D171; Closes #368). `firecrawl-anydoc` and `pdf-inspector` join the default extras, the venv directory's name hashes the extras beside the ready check so this venv and main's never rebuild each other, and a `YI_KERNEL_PYTHON` may lack the two wheels. A file whose head carries a zip, OLE2, `%PDF-` or `{\rtf` marker goes to the venv's python once, under a 256 MiB ceiling (1 GiB with `pages=`), a 30 s timeout and, on macOS, seatbelt with no network and the cache dir as its one writable root (a user and network namespace on Linux where `unshare` allows one); the converter sniffs the content, so a `.docx` named `.txt` converts. csv is read as text; Latin-1 and UTF-16 text is decoded with a note; an image is named as one; a NUL-bearing file that is no document is refused as binary. The Markdown goes to `~/.yi/converted/<stem>-<key>.<hash>.<kind>.md` (dir mode 0700, copy read-only), keyed by the source's path, length and byte hash and by the Markdown's own hash: a saved original misses, a forged copy is converted again, parallel first reads each stage under their own name, and copies older than 30 days or past 256 MiB in total are evicted. The copy is read through the ordinary window under the original's path (no path outside the working tree reaches the model), unclipped, with a heading outline when capped and a heading section for `find=`; `edit` and `write` refuse any existing binary document or NUL-bearing file and any path under the copy dir, while RTF and decoded text stay writable. PDF pages arrive under `[page N]` markers, `pages="3-5"` reads only those, and pages without a text layer are named and left out; only a PDF with no text page is refused. Spreadsheets lose empty rows and cell padding and gain a `[sheets: …]` line. A glob charges the Markdown, not the bytes on disk. OCR is pinned to `reject` and nothing reaches the hosted mode. The bootstrap records the wheel's formats and aliases as `documentFormats` beside a hash of the probe, refreshed at the next readiness check when the probe changes; `read`'s description lists exactly those, asked again on every request, or nothing when the venv recorded none; `bash` names `read` when a command reaches for `pdftotext`, `pandoc` or `textutil`. Round three, from playing Yi on two terminal-bench tasks and a coursework folder: a PDF page with a detected table or two columns arrives as plain text in reading order under a note that the conversion is lossy (a row stays whole; columns read left then right; headings restored), running headers are left out once, decks carry `[slide N]` markers, a one-page PDF carries no page marker, sheets lose empty columns and point a sheet past 500 rows at `pandas.read_excel` (`openpyxl` joins the extras), `grep` searches documents through their Markdown and names what it could not search, a glob converts only while it has room, a copy's first look is 12 KB with its outline first (shallowest levels, sampled, repeated titles once), `find=` folds typographic quotes, a copy adds no code refs, the kernel mutes pip's version notice, and the ipython description names `anydoc`, `pdf_inspector` and pandas. The math task's answer keys now parse 48/48 from `read`'s text alone (47 before), and the schedule question that came back with a neighbour's slot now comes back right. Two dogfood runs over 141 real documents from this machine: 118 converted (cold median about 110 ms, 5.3 s at most on a 239 MB Latin-1 csv), 17 refused with a stated reason (8 PDFs with no text page, 1 past the ceiling, 6 of anydoc's resource or malformed refusals, 1 encrypted, 1 Word lock file), 2 decoded, 4 read as the text they were. The plan's phase gate (docs/plans/2026-09-09-anydoc-and-pdf-inspector.md §9) was waived by the owner, so all three phases ship here; §12 there records the departures. Tests: `documents::{a_docx_reads_as_markdown_through_the_read_window, the_copy_is_reused_until_the_original_changes, write_and_edit_refuse_a_converted_original, a_scanned_pdf_is_refused_with_its_reason, the_content_names_the_format_not_the_extension, a_binary_that_is_no_document_keeps_the_plain_error, an_rtf_file_converts_though_it_is_seven_bit_text, a_partly_scanned_pdf_reads_its_text_pages_and_names_the_rest, parallel_first_reads_of_one_document_all_succeed, a_file_past_the_ceiling_is_refused_before_the_converter_runs, a_hanging_converter_is_stopped_at_the_timeout_and_on_cancel, a_document_with_a_unicode_name_converts, a_document_whose_name_is_not_utf8_converts, a_tampered_copy_is_converted_again, write_refuses_an_unread_document_and_allows_text_formats, text_that_is_not_utf8_is_decoded_rather_than_refused, a_glob_reads_documents_whole_and_counts_their_markdown, markdown_gets_an_outline_when_capped_and_find_returns_its_section, a_spreadsheet_loses_its_empty_rows_columns_and_padding, a_large_sheet_points_at_a_pandas_route_that_works, a_pdf_table_and_columns_arrive_as_reading_order_text_under_a_note, running_headers_are_left_out_once_and_body_lines_kept, a_one_page_pdf_carries_no_page_marker, a_deck_reads_slide_by_slide_with_its_notes, a_long_document_opens_with_its_outline_and_a_short_first_look, find_matches_typographic_quotes_and_adds_no_code_refs, grep_searches_documents_through_their_markdown_and_never_rewrites_them, a_glob_lists_what_it_does_not_convert_and_why}`, `prompt_drift::read_names_exactly_the_formats_the_installed_wheel_converts`, yi-tools `documents::{the_description_claims_only_the_recorded_formats, an_unbuilt_venv_is_named_beside_the_plain_error, the_description_follows_a_venv_built_mid_session, bash_points_a_habitual_converter_at_read}`, `bootstrap::tests::{the_default_venv_is_keyed_by_the_ready_check, the_converter_reads_back_the_formats_its_venv_recorded, a_kernel_python_may_lack_the_converter_wheels}`. growth +14511: measured at the push under D171; this version adds about 1520 src lines, the converter, its refusals, the copy cache and the Markdown resolver in one yi-tools module and one resolver, since a `convert` tool (a prompt cost on every request) and the in-process crate (3.42 MiB) were both weighed and refused. |
| 0.206.0 | 2026-09-09 | A stream that dies mid-turn is settled from the generation record (amends D79 and D163; Closes #331). An upstream drop after the first chunk (a 502 from a slow provider, D146's retry case) ended the turn as an error with `unknown` usage, and under D79 that voided the trial's cost: the 07:10 foodstuff job carried one and the slice driver refused cargo on it, the fifth stop of the night. The completions path now settles such a turn from the record the way 0.201.0 settles a cut, behind one `settle_from_record`, and emits its error with the tokens it cost; a stream that died before any chunk has no record and stays unknown. The retry rule is unchanged (`nothing_delivered` reads the same usage). Tests: `openai_mapper::a_stream_that_dies_mid_turn_keeps_the_usage_it_reported`. growth +12995: measured at the push under D168; this version adds about 15 src lines. |
| 0.205.0 | 2026-09-09 | A cut stream ends as a length `Done` (amends D163; Closes #327). After the pump returned at the cut flag, `ChunkMapper::finish` saw no finish reason and emitted `Stream ended without finish_reason` as an error; the loop's drain returned on it, the cut message was built from the pre-stream partial, and the usage 0.201.0 had settled from the generation record was never emitted. 0.202.0 and 0.204.0 widened the window and the drain around a `Done` that did not exist; the 02:56 run on 0.204.0 still read `unknown: true` on its cut turn. A cut is a length stop by definition: `ChunkMapper::cut` marks the finish and the stop reason before the settle and the finish, so the `Done` carries the record's usage and the id. Tests: `openai_mapper::a_cut_stream_finishes_as_a_length_done_with_its_id`; the loop pins hold. growth +12973: measured at the push under D168; this version adds about 10 src lines. |
| 0.204.0 | 2026-09-09 | The cut drain is bounded by its deadline alone (amends D163; Closes #325). 0.201.0 read at most 256 events after the cut flag; the provider channel holds 256 and a fast upstream has that many thinking deltas queued ahead of the `Done` at the moment of the cut, so the drain gave up on the cap, the cut message was built from the pre-stream partial with no `responseId`, and the settled usage the provider had fetched was thrown away: every photonic cut turn of the 01:48 job read `unknown: true` and the driver stopped a fourth time. The 45-second deadline is now the only bound; the deltas after the cut are bounded by the channel and by the pump, which stops reading at the flag. The loop's faux stream now hands its thinking over in 32-char deltas, so `loop_events::a_cut_turn_keeps_the_usage_the_provider_settled` finds the `Done` behind more than 800 of them. growth +12961: measured at the push under D168; this version removes about 5 src lines. |
| 0.203.0 | 2026-09-09 | A reasoning cut is not a length strike (D168, amends D153 and D162; Closes #323). Both finished photonic attempts of the 01:30 slice run reasoned past 48,000 chars three times in a row after one or two tool turns and D153's third strike ended them with no file written; the artifact steer never fired, being keyed to the third tool turn. Cuts now have their own count of twelve (`CUT_STOP_AT`), the re-drive from the second consecutive cut names the write (`CUT_REDRIVE_TEXT`), and the coupling counts a cut turn beside a tool turn toward the steer. Ordinary length stops keep the rule of three. Tests: `loop_events::consecutive_cuts_re_drive_past_the_third_and_name_the_write`, `a_bare_length_stop_is_re_driven_twice_then_ends_on_the_third` (unchanged), `todo_coupling::cut_turns_count_toward_the_artifact_steer`. growth +12961: measured at the push under D167; this version adds about 25 src lines. |
| 0.202.0 | 2026-09-09 | The cut settlement waits for the generation record (amends D163; Closes #321). 0.201.0 tried the record at 0.5, 2, 5 and 10 s after the drop and the loop drained 15 s for the provider's `Done`; measured at 01:20, the record is a 404 at 2, 5 and 8 s and present at 10 s, so the last try landed on the edge and the drain expired with it: the 01:07 slice run recorded its cut turns with no `responseId` and `unknown: true` again and the driver stopped after html-js-filter a third time. The tries are now at 3, 8, 16 and 28 s and the drain 45 s, paid only on a cut (three turns in nine html-js-filter trials tonight); a trial whose spend cannot be measured is paid in full and counts for nothing. Tests: the 0.201.0 pins hold unchanged. growth +12935: measured at the push under D163; this version adds no src lines. |
| 0.201.0 | 2026-09-09 | A turn cut at the reasoning budget is settled from the generation record (amends D163; Closes #319). The cut drops the stream before its usage chunk, so the turn was recorded `unknown` with a chars/4 estimate, and under D79 an unknown turn voids the trial's cost: the 2026-09-08 slice run stopped after its first task on `spend cannot be measured`. Measured on OpenRouter: a stream dropped at 12,010 chars is billed for what was generated (3,089 completion tokens, $0.00077) and `/generation?id=` serves the record within 20 s. The completions path now fetches that record after a cut (four tries over ten seconds) and its `Done` carries the measured tokens and cost; the loop drains to that `Done` after setting the cut flag (15 s, 256 events at most) and `cut_message` keeps a settled usage, leaving the estimate marked `unknown` only when no record comes. Other providers keep the estimate. Tests: `loop_events::a_cut_turn_keeps_the_usage_the_provider_settled`, `a_reasoning_spiral_is_cut_at_the_char_budget_and_re_driven` (the estimate stays unknown), `settle::tests::a_generation_record_becomes_a_measured_usage`. growth +12934: measured at the push under D167; this version adds about 60 src lines. |
| 0.200.0 | 2026-09-08 | The forge is driven with `fgj` instead of `tea` (D167, amends D122; Closes #312). tea's actions verbs asked for routes this Forgejo does not serve and its open list needed a tsv field spec to parse; fgj is Forgejo-native and `gh`-shaped. The lane adapter opens with `fgj pr create`, reads the open list as `--json` rows by `head.ref`, polls through `fgj api --hostname <origin host>` (`host_of`) and merges with `--merge-method=merge`; `scripts/forge_pr.py` sends bodies on stdin and reads a 404 from stderr; the tracking script, the metadata gate, `docs/FORGE.md` and the two forge skills print fgj fix lines. Tests: `lanes::an_open_pull_request_is_found_by_head` (fgj rows by head ref), `lanes::the_origin_host_is_read_from_ssh_and_https_urls`; the three scripts' selfchecks. growth +12794: measured at the push under D166; this version adds about 10 src lines. |
